# Graph Report - SoporteCero  (2026-11-20)

## Corpus Check
- 975 files · ~2,032,693 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 616 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .pak 446, .hyb 104, (none) 25)
- WARNING: 616 of 1965 files seen (31%) have no supported extractor and are missing from this graph entirely. It may look complete while representing only part of the codebase.

## Summary
- 11134 nodes · 14808 edges · 952 communities (902 shown, 50 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 397 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b85e39bf`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
